<details>
<summary><span style="font-size: 1.1em; font-weight: 600;">Accent Ranking (3 flavors)</span></summary>

Rank = how important the highlight groups actually rendering that color are in real Neovim usage (captured via headless Neovim, weighted by group tier — see `_group_tier_weight` in `get-colors.py`). Split into **Used** (referenced by this repo's own SEG/DC_\*/GC_\* roles) and **Unused**. `Blended`/`Original` mark `--blend`-generated `{name}__blend` entries and the color they were reshaded from.

Split into **Used** (referenced by this repo's own SEG/DC_\*/GC_\* roles) and **Unused**. `Blended`/`Original` mark `--blend`-generated `{name}__blend` entries and the color they were reshaded from.

<details>
<summary><span style="font-size: 1.1em; font-weight: 600;">Accent Ranking (4 flavors)</span></summary>

Rank = how important the highlight groups actually rendering that color are in real Neovim usage (captured via headless Neovim, weighted by group tier — see `_group_tier_weight` in `get-colors.py`). Split into **Used** (referenced by this repo's own SEG/DC_\*/GC_\* roles) and **Unused**. `Blended`/`Original` mark `--blend`-generated `{name}__blend` entries and the color they were reshaded from.

<details>
<summary><span style="font-size: 1.1em; font-weight: 600;">Accent Ranking (2 flavors)</span></summary>

Rank = how important the highlight groups actually rendering that color are in real Neovim usage (captured via headless Neovim, weighted by group tier — see `_group_tier_weight` in `get-colors.py`). Split into **Used** (referenced by this repo's own SEG/DC_\*/GC_\* roles) and **Unused**. `Blended`/`Original` mark `--blend`-generated `{name}__blend` entries and the color they were reshaded from.

Rank = how important the highlight groups actually rendering that color are in real Neovim usage (captured via headless Neovim, weighted by group tier — see `_group_tier_weight` in `get-colors.py`). Split into **Used** (referenced by this repo's own SEG/DC_\*/GC_\* roles) and **Unused**. `Blended`/`Original` mark `--blend`-generated `{name}__blend` entries and the color they were reshaded from.

<a id="kanagawa-accent-ranking"></a>
<details>
<summary><span style="font-size: 1.1em; font-weight: 600;">Accent Ranking (3 flavors)</span></summary>

Rank = how important the highlight groups actually rendering that color are in real Neovim usage (captured via headless Neovim, weighted by group tier — see `_group_tier_weight` in `get-colors.py`). Split into **Used** (referenced by this repo's own SEG/DC_\*/GC_\* roles) and **Unused**. `Blended`/`Original` mark `--blend`-generated `{name}__blend` entries and the color they were reshaded from.

<a id="monokai-accent-ranking"></a>
<details>
<summary><span style="font-size: 1.1em; font-weight: 600;">Accent Ranking (7 flavors)</span></summary>

Rank = how important the highlight groups actually rendering that color are in real Neovim usage (captured via headless Neovim, weighted by group tier — see `_group_tier_weight` in `get-colors.py`). Split into **Used** (referenced by this repo's own SEG/DC_\*/GC_\* roles) and **Unused**. `Blended`/`Original` mark `--blend`-generated `{name}__blend` entries and the color they were reshaded from.

Split into **Used** (referenced by this repo's own SEG/DC_\*/GC_\* roles) and **Unused**. `Blended`/`Original` mark `--blend`-generated `{name}__blend` entries and the color they were reshaded from.

<details>
<summary><span style="font-size: 1.1em; font-weight: 600;">Accent Ranking (4 flavors)</span></summary>

Rank = how important the highlight groups actually rendering that color are in real Neovim usage (captured via headless Neovim, weighted by group tier — see `_group_tier_weight` in `get-colors.py`). Split into **Used** (referenced by this repo's own SEG/DC_\*/GC_\* roles) and **Unused**. `Blended`/`Original` mark `--blend`-generated `{name}__blend` entries and the color they were reshaded from.

Split into **Used** (referenced by this repo's own SEG/DC_\*/GC_\* roles) and **Unused**. `Blended`/`Original` mark `--blend`-generated `{name}__blend` entries and the color they were reshaded from.
